# Attribution: FreeCAD parts library

The parts run in this directory are from the [FreeCAD parts library](https://github.com/FreeCAD/FreeCAD-library), at commit `f5ca01cdcaa2f69b9295a6382719c2d90628780c`, licensed under [CC BY 3.0](https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by/3.0/) by their authors. The author of each is the person who added it to the library, as its README directs. The files are not redistributed here: `fetch.py` fetches them from the library. They are used unchanged; where a result or a case is derived from a part, it says so and credits the part as "modified from" it.

2442 parts. Parts the library holds under other terms, or that are vendors' or third parties' models, are not used (see `status` and `reason` in `parts.tsv`).
